Qatar & Middle East — Daily·Weekly Media Monitor

Twice a day (07:00 and 15:30 Qatar time), major coverage from Qatari, Iranian, other major countries' and Korean outlets is compiled into AI issue summaries with source links. A list of the latest reports from relevant institutions is also available for reference lower on the page, and every Sunday a weekly report recapping the past week is published. 🧭 Key issue briefs AI summary
Issue 1

⚔️ Reignition of US-Iran Escalation and Clashes Near Jordan and the Strait of Hormuz

Key summary
  • Iran attacked a US military base in Jordan, reigniting the US-Iran armed conflict that had been dormant for weeks; US President Trump vowed to 'strongly strike' Iran in retaliation
  • Iran claimed that a US attack on Larak Island killed many soldiers and civilians, demanded UN Security Council intervention, and Iran's military warned it would respond without hesitation to any further attacks
  • A senior IRGC official claimed that over 90% of its strategic missile stockpile remains intact, and an IRGC official stated that 'deterrence is the top priority, and whether it's war or negotiation is a secondary matter'
  • The GCC condemned Iran's attack on Jordan, and Qatar's Ministry of Foreign Affairs also strongly condemned Iran's attacks on Jordan and the UAE as violations of international law and the UN Charter and a 'dangerous escalation,' calling for de-escalation

Issue 2

🚢 Tanker Attack in the Strait of Hormuz and Maritime Safety Crisis

Key summary
  • The UK Maritime Trade Operations (UKMTO) confirmed that a tanker exiting the Strait of Hormuz near Oman was struck by three projectiles, cited as a factor heightening escalation concerns
  • Analysis suggests the US is struggling to secure control of the Strait of Hormuz due to difficulties countering mine warfare; however, according to Reuters data, cargo ship transit volume through the Strait of Hormuz has not yet shown a clear change
  • Yemen's military stated it repelled a Houthi rebel boat attack near islands in the Red Sea, and Yemeni government forces carried out drone strikes on Houthi positions on multiple fronts

Issue 3

🛢️ Surge in International Oil Prices and Impact on Korean Industry

Key summary
  • With the resumption of US-Iran clashes and Trump's hardline retaliation warning, international oil prices rose again as concerns over Middle East supply disruptions resurfaced, turning oil prices upward after weeks of stability
  • Amid escalation concerns, Asian stock markets broadly turned weaker, with Hong Kong's Hang Seng Index (-0.59%), the H-share Index (-0.63%), and China's Shanghai Composite Index (-0.16%) opening lower

🤝 International Pressure on Iran and Diplomatic Maneuvering

Key summary
  • US Treasury Secretary Bessent launched 'Operation Economic Outcast' to isolate and pressure Iran internationally, and urged allies at the G20 to join in isolating Iran, but cracks emerged in relations with allies over tariff issues
  • Chinese President Xi Jinping showcased ties with Russia, India, and Iran at the Shanghai Cooperation Organisation (SCO) summit, suggesting that the US economic war against Iran has hit its limits; Iran's Foreign Ministry criticized the EU for 'surrendering its sovereignty' by aligning with US sanctions on Iran

Issue 5

🕌 Israeli Minister's Incursion into Al-Aqsa Mosque and Qatar's Condemnation

Key summary
  • Far-right Israeli National Security Minister Ben-Gvir entered Al-Aqsa Mosque (the third holiest site in Islam) in Jerusalem with settlers, violating a decades-long status quo agreement, under the protection of Israeli occupation forces
  • Qatar's Ministry of Foreign Affairs issued a statement strongly condemning the incursion into Al-Aqsa Mosque by extremist forces including Ben-Gvir
  • In the West Bank, Israeli forces continue demolishing Palestinian homes and expanding settlements, with reports confirming that settlers in Nablus, Hebron, and other areas are forcibly clearing land for new outposts

Issue 6

💥 Situation in Gaza and Lebanon, and Qatar's Diplomatic Statements

Key summary
  • Israeli airstrikes killed at least 5 people in the Gaza Strip; in southern Lebanon, a child was injured and later died from the explosion of cluster munition remnants left by Israeli forces
  • Lebanese President Aoun asked Greece to help support the implementation of the US-brokered agreement with Israel, and the Speaker of Lebanon's Parliament stated he would not relinquish the right to file charges over Israel's 'crimes of genocide'
  • Qatar issued a series of condemnation statements regarding Iran's attacks on Jordan and the UAE, the incursion into Al-Aqsa Mosque, and the situation in Niger's capital, expressing a diplomatic stance calling for regional de-escalation

This page automatically collects and summarizes public news coverage and material from relevant institutions across Qatar, Iran, other major countries and Korea. News is gathered by querying Google News RSS feeds, Naver News search, and outlet RSS (Al Jazeera, BBC, etc.) with keywords such as Qatar, the Middle East and energy; in-depth reports are collected directly from the publication boards and RSS of domestic and international research institutes, international organizations and consultancies (supplemented by news search targeting those publishers), keeping only analyses and reports on the Middle East situation, the Iran war and their industrial, supply-chain and energy repercussions. The report list accumulates items published since the war's outbreak (2026-02-28), split into domestic and overseas and ordered by publication date (newest first). Collection and summarization rely on each outlet's publicly available headline and snippet; for subscription or paywalled outlets, the full article can be opened through the provided source link only after the reader signs in or subscribes to that outlet directly. The collected articles are automatically clustered and summarized by issue, and translated into English and Arabic, by Anthropic Claude (Sonnet 5) AI. The whole process runs and publishes automatically twice a day (07:00 and 15:30 Qatar time) via GitHub Actions. The Qatar government activity section separately curates only official statements, announcements and actions on the war and regional situation by Qatar government and state entities — the Emir, the Prime Minister, the ministries of Foreign Affairs, Defense and Interior, the Government Communications Office (GCO), QatarEnergy and the state news agency (QNA). Owing to automated collection some articles or reports may be missed, so please re-verify important issues against the original sources and further searches.
- Embassy of the Republic of Korea in Qatar, Commercial Section · KOTRA Doha
